  const IXP_CACHE_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags';
  const IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags-ts';
  const IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  function returnIXPDataFromCacheIfValid() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const rawFlags = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      if (!rawFlags || rawFlags === '{}') {
        return null;
      }

      const parsedFlags = JSON.parse(rawFlags);
      if (!parsedFlags || typeof parsedFlags !== 'object') {
        return null;
      }

      const rawTimestamp = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      const now = Date.now();

      if (!rawTimestamp) {
        return null;
      }

      const cachedAt = Number(rawTimestamp);
      if (Number.isFinite(cachedAt) && now - cachedAt < IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
        return parsedFlags;
      }

      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to read IXP cache from sessionStorage:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }
   // adding this dummy function here to avoid page break of buy/internet page. that page is calling this function
  function unmountGlobalNavScripts() {
    console.log('unmountGlobalNavScripts called latest..');
    const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
    if (isNativeApp) {
      const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
      gnavHeaderId?.remove();
    }
  }
  function getMeganavCookie(lookup) {
    let nameEQ = 'accessDomain' + '=';
    const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    if (typeof lookup !== 'undefined') {
      nameEQ = lookup + '=';
    }
    for (let c of ca) {
      while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
        c = c.substring(1, c.length);
      }
      if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
        return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
      }
    }
  }
  const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
  if (isNativeApp) {
    const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
    gnavHeaderId?.remove();
  } else {
    // Fire CMS nav data fetch immediately so it runs in parallel with JS bundle loading.
    // csrInitialization.fetchCMSDataCSR reads this Promise from window instead of issuing a new call.
    if (!window.__megaNavCmsPrefetch) {
      window.__megaNavCmsPrefetch = fetch(
        '/msapi/idp-content-orchestration/v1/scms/support/web-support/meganav/consumer/header',
        { headers: { Accept: 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      ).then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
       .catch(function() { return null; });
    }
    // Fire IXP feature-flag fetch immediately in parallel with JS bundle loading.
    // featureFlagApi.fetchIxpFeatureFlagData reads this Promise from window instead of issuing a new call.
    if (!window.__megaNavIxpPrefetch) {
      const cachedFlags = returnIXPDataFromCacheIfValid();
      if (cachedFlags) {
        window.__megaNavIxpPrefetch = Promise.resolve(cachedFlags);
        window.__megaNavIxpPrefetch.fetchedFromCache = true;
      } else {
      window.__megaNavIxpPrefetch = fetch(
        '/msapi/ixp-allocation-manager-service/v1/allocations?app=idp-globalnav',
        { headers: { Accept: 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }
      ).then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
       .catch(function() { return null; });
      }
    }
    // Process scripts based on the nav version
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      let scripts = document.querySelectorAll(
        'script[src*="/nav"][type="text/plain"]',
      );
      console.log('@@@ [LOAD_SCRIPTS] scripts:', scripts);
      if (scripts && scripts.length > 0) {
        scripts.forEach((script) => {
          const newScript = document.createElement('script');

          newScript.type = 'module';
          newScript.setAttribute('defer', '');
          newScript.src = script.src;

          console.log('@@@ [LOAD_SCRIPTS] Replacing script:', script.src);
          script.parentNode.replaceChild(newScript, script);
        });
      }
    });
  }

'meganav-featureflags';\n  const IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags-ts';\n  const IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;\n  function returnIXPDataFromCacheIfValid() {\n    if (typeof window === 'undefined') {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const rawFlags = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);\n      if (!rawFlags || rawFlags === '{}') {\n        return null;\n      }\n\n      const parsedFlags = JSON.parse(rawFlags);\n      if (!parsedFlags || typeof parsedFlags !== 'object') {\n        return null;\n      }\n\n      const rawTimestamp = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);\n      const now = Date.now();\n\n      if (!rawTimestamp) {\n        return null;\n      }\n\n      const cachedAt = Number(rawTimestamp);\n      if (Number.isFinite(cachedAt) \u0026\u0026 now - cachedAt \u003c IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS) {\n        return parsedFlags;\n      }\n\n      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);\n      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);\n      return null;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn('Failed to read IXP cache from sessionStorage:', error);\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n   // adding this dummy function here to avoid page break of buy/internet page. that page is calling this function\n  function unmountGlobalNavScripts() {\n    console.log('unmountGlobalNavScripts called latest..');\n    const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';\n    if (isNativeApp) {\n      const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');\n      gnavHeaderId?.remove();\n    }\n  }\n  function getMeganavCookie(lookup) {\n    let nameEQ = 'accessDomain' + '=';\n    const ca =
